# Provenance map: Paper A (results)

Generated by `qa/tools/make_provenance.py` from `papers/A_results/SECTION_SCOPE.md`, `qa/claims_register.csv`, `qa/sources_register.csv` and `qa/literature_register.csv`. Do not edit by hand; rerun the script.

**How to read a row.** Each point of a section has a row per fact. A fact is a claim the scope cites (statement shortened), or a file or register cell the scope names. The chain runs: claim id, tier and status; output file and locator; script; data source ids with kind and status; the source's own origin; literature ids with read status.

One-word statuses. Claim: **two-route** (register status independently verified, tier A), **one-route** (self-checked, or independently verified at tier B or C where one route is the requirement), **none** (unverified or withdrawn). Source: **verified** or **unverified** (register status). Literature: **page-level** (read to page level), **read** (read, including targeted reads), **weak** (reported second-hand, record only, or not reached). Files: **ok** or **MISSING**.

Contents: [Section 1](#section-1); [Section 2](#section-2); [Section 3](#section-3); [Section 4](#section-4); [Section 5](#section-5); [Section 6](#section-6); [Section 7](#section-7); [Section 8](#section-8); [Section 9](#section-9); [Section 10](#section-10); [Section 11](#section-11); [Section 12](#section-12); [Tallies](#tallies); [Broken links](#broken-links); [Unparsed](#unparsed).

## Section 6

### The mechanism

#### Point 1: The rule: cropland and pasture in nearly fixed proportion; capacity set by the land type that runs short...

Evidence cell: A346; A338
